### Query 30
**User query:**
> Reply context:
- Replied-to role: assistant
- Replied-to message: roughly, but not exactly.
With 4 Medusa heads, the usual setup is:
• The base LM head predicts token t₁ — the next token.
• Medusa head 1 predicts t₂.
• Medusa head 2 predicts t₃.
• Medusa head 3 predicts t₄.
• Medusa head 4 predicts t₅.
So it can propose up to 5 tokens total—the normal next token plus four future-token predictions—from one backbone forward pass. Medusa: Simple LLM Inference Acceleration Framework with Multiple Decoding Heads
However, the important caveat is that the 4 future tokens are speculative. They are generated from the same current hidden state, so they do not perfectly condition on one another. Medusa uses tree-based candidate generation and then has the base model …

Current user message:
So explain to me clearly how the verifier works based on the three attention after the attention scores of each sequence are produced by the three attention.
